- **zerion-agent-management**: Manage Zerion agent tokens and security policies — the primitives for autonomous trading and signing. Create / list / use / revoke agent tokens; create / list / show / delete policies (chain locks, allowlists, transfer/approval gates, expiry). Use whenever the user asks to set up an agent token, configure a policy, or enable autonomous trading. Required by `zerion-trading` and `zerion-sign`. ([danielAsaboro/aegis-leash](https://github.com/danielAsaboro/aegis-leash/tree/HEAD/skills/zerion-agent-management))

- **zero-inflated-count-model-fitting**: Decision support for selecting between NB and ZINB noise models before denoising a scRNA-seq count matrix, when sparsity/dropout structure suggests possible zero-inflation. - **zero-inflated-count-modeling**: Use when count-per-bin data (e.g., HDF counts per 25 bp genome bin) shows far more zeros than a Poisson predicts and you need to choose among ZIP, Poisson, and NB emissions for a background state. - **zero-inflated-count-simulation**: Use when you need synthetic zero-inflated, over-dispersed scRNA-seq counts with known true parameters (ln(mu), pi) to benchmark a model's robustness to misspecification of K, V, or dispersion. - **zero-inflated-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when scRNA-seq count data show dropout-driven zero inflation and PCA components appear driven by technical effects (sequencing depth, detection rate), and you need a low-dimensional representation reflecting biology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/zero-inflated-dimensionality-reduction))

- **zero-inflated-gaussian-model-fitting**: Use when metagenomic/metatranscriptomic gene-cluster abundance or expression tables (e.g. RPKM counts from BiG-MAP mapping) have many zero-inflated values and you need parametric differential testing between grouped samples. Output Strukturiertes Kopfdatenblatt mit Vollständigkeitsprüfung und Zeugnisart-Einordnung als Eingabe für alle Folge-Analyse-Skills. Abgrenzung zu zeugnisart-erkennung und notenrelevante-saetze-identifizieren.
